--- PHYSICAL PAGE 2 ---
SERVICE SUMMARY – Introduction
This document summarizes all transit services operated by the Toronto Transit Commission for the
period shown. It includes all rapid transit, streetcar, bus, and community bus routes.
For each route, the summary lists the route name and destinations, the garage or carhouse
responsible for the service, key service details, and first and last trip times. Scheduled headway is
shown for each route, along with a combined or average headway where multiple branches operate.
The summary also includes the number and type of vehicles assigned, the round-trip driving time,
total terminal time, and the average operating speed of the route (based on driving time only and
excluding terminal time).
The first and last trip times shown reflect trips that operate over the full length of each branch. Some
routes may have earlier or later trips that operate over only part of the route; these partial trips are
not included.
Notes are included for routes that interline with other routes, are temporarily operating on  scheduled
diversion, or are temporarily operated by buses instead of streetcars.
Service frequencies are shown in minutes and seconds. Running times and terminal times are
shown in minutes. Distances are shown in kilometres, and speeds are shown in kilometres per hour.
This document also provides a summary of vehicles and trains operated by time period, a complete
list of TTC routes (shown alphabetically, numerically, and by division), a list of connections at rapid
transit stations, and a list of transit vehicles.
This document is available on the TTC website here, and on the internal TTC Intranet (Internal-TTC
access only) here.
Service-Related Abbreviations
Avg spd .... Average speed (km/h) NB ............. Northbound
Dep ........... Departure SB ............. Southbound
RT ............. Round trip EB ............. Eastbound
Term ......... Terminal time WB ............ Westbound
Veh type ... Vehicle type

Division Abbreviations
Arw ........... Arrow Road Mal ............ Malvern Les/Rus ..... Leslie/Russell
Bir ............. Birchmount McN .......... McNicoll Wil ............. Wilson Bus
DanSub .... Danforth Subway MtD ........... Mount Dennis WilSub ....... Wilson Subway
Egl ............ Eglinton Qsy ........... Queensway W-T ........... Wheel-Trans
EMSF ....... Eglinton MSF                  FMSF ........ Finch West MSF Ron ........... Roncesvalles

Vehicle Abbreviations
6carT ........ Six-car train of T- or TR-series 23-metre subway cars (Lines 1 and 2)
4carT ........ Four-car train of TR-series 23-metre subway cars (Line 4)
LFLRV ...... 30-metre low-floor light-rail vehicle (Flexity Freedom – Line 5)
LFsc .......... 30-metre low-floor streetcar (Flexity Outlook)
LRV .......... 48-metre light-rail vehicle (Alstom Citadis Spirit – Line 6)
Bus ........... 12-metre bus (Orion VII, Nova LFS)
ABus ......... 18-metre articulated bus (Nova LFS Artic)
CB ............ 8-metre Community Bus (Friendly Bus)
WT ............ 8-metre Wheel-Trans bus (Friendly Bus, ProMaster)

Time Periods
Service is shown by time period. The times shown are guidelines and may vary by route.

SERVICE OUTSIDE TORONTO

Monday-Friday: 7 BATHURST, 29 DUFFERIN, 129 MCCOWAN NORTH, 902 MARKHAM RD EXPRESS, 927 HIGHWAY 27 EXPRESS, 929 DUFFERIN EXPRESS, 939 FINCH EXPRESS, 941 KEELE EXPRESS, 943 KENNEDY EXPRESS, 953 STEELES EAST EXPRESS

Vehicle numbers include vehicles required for City, Metrolinx and TTC construction.
